I'll think I've finished a customer ticket, and then... well, later I'll notice I typed the wrong account number. Or maybe I skipped a required box. Just... missing one little instruction, and the whole thing comes back to me. So... yeah. It happens.\n\nDoctor: Do you experience restlessness or fidgeting at home or in social situations, not just at work?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I suppose so. Um... even with quiet stuff. Like, if I'm trying to read at home or... sitting through a movie with my wife. I don't really last long before I'm... shifting around, or making comments. Or I just get up to find something to do. So... yeah, it's not just at work.\n\nDoctor: Have you always felt this way, even when you were a kid?\n\nPatient: I guess so. Um... yeah, it's been a long time. Even before I started this job. I remember... well, my parents used to show me my old report cards from grade school. The teachers would always write the same sort of things. That I was a bright kid, but... careless. Or that I talked out of turn a lot. They were always having to remind me to just... stay on task and finish my work. So... yeah, I suppose it's always been there.\n\nDoctor: Did you have more energy than your siblings or friends when you were a kid?\n\nPatient: I don't really know. Um... maybe? I mean, I guess kids usually have a lot of energy. I don't remember paying much attention to how I compared to my friends or anything back then. So... I'm not sure.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had trouble waiting for your turn in conversations or interrupted others while they were speaking?\n\nPatient: Um... sometimes, I guess. Even if they aren't finished talking, or... before anyone actually asks me. So... yeah, that happens.\n\nDoctor: As a child, did you often run, climb, or play more vigorously than your peers?\n\nPatient: I don't really remember. It's been a long time since I was a kid. I was just... a kid playing, I guess. I couldn't tell you if I ran around more than anyone else.\n\nDoctor: Do you experience these restless or fidgety feelings in all aspects of your life, including when you're alone?\n\nPatient: I haven't really noticed. Um... when I'm by myself, I'm usually just at home. I don't really pay attention to whether I'm fidgeting or not. So... I'm not sure.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced these symptoms consistently throughout your adult life as well?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I suppose so. I mean... looking back, it's been around long before I started this job. Even in grade school, I remember... well, my teachers used to write on my report cards that I was bright but... careless. They said I talked out of turn a lot. And they always had to remind me to just stay with my work. So... it's just always been there, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Have these symptoms caused you significant distress or problems in your daily life?\n\nPatient: I suppose... maybe a little. Mostly at work, I guess. Um... it's started showing up in my reviews lately. My supervisors keep pointing out... well, unfinished tickets. And some mistakes. They say it slows the team down. I mean, I'm trying hard, and... I get along fine with the customers. But yeah... I guess it causes some issues there.\n\nDoctor: Have these symptoms caused problems in your relationships with family or friends?\n\nPatient: Um... no, I don't think so. Things are mostly fine. I kind of just... keep to myself a lot outside of work anyway, so... no, no real problems there.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever been in a situation where your restlessness or fidgeting caused you to have trouble getting along with someone?\n\nPatient: Um... no. That's about it.\n\nDoctor: Do you often act on impulse without thinking things through?\n\nPatient: Not really. I mean... I don't think so. I usually know what I'm doing before I do it. I try to look calm, but my foot is usually bouncing under the desk. Or I'll be... clicking a pen during a meeting and not really notice I'm doing it. I'll think I've finished a customer ticket, and then... well, later I'll notice I typed the wrong account number. Or maybe I skipped a required box. Just... missing one little instruction, and the whole thing comes back to me. So... yeah. It happens.\n\nDoctor: Do you experience restlessness or fidgeting at home or in social situations, not just at work?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I suppose so. Um... even with quiet stuff. So... yeah, I suppose it's always been there.\n\nDoctor: Did you have more energy than your siblings or friends when you were a kid?\n\nPatient: I don't really know. Um... maybe? I'm not sure.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had trouble waiting for your turn in conversations or interrupted others while they were speaking?\n\nPatient: Um... sometimes, I guess.
